阶段式教师纠错 → 学生自主检查

单图开发、单图测试；同一任务内持续加载前一 .blend 并应用增量补丁。开发教师纠错与测试学生自查是过程指导，均不是外部质量评分。模型原始记录为英文，页面说明为中文；没有自动排名。

如何完整下载：Git 核心文件与大附件

服务器本地完整实验目录可直接打开本页。若只下载 GitHub 分支的核心文件，完整日志、对象清单或 .blend 链接可能不在该下载中；请到 仓库 Releases 查找本轮 stagewise_teaching_student_selfcheck_pilot 的完整归档，按包内说明解压并保持原有相对目录，再打开 reports/index.html。不要只单独下载 HTML。

这里只说明完整包的使用方式，不代表附件已经上传或核验。上传状态以本轮 artifacts_index／上传收据为准；尚无确认收据时，完整产物只保证保存在服务器本地。

A 开发教学 · B 规则迁移 · C 学生测试

所有人工意见默认未填写。点击图片可打开原始大小；联系图只缩放显示，不改变原始文件。

A．D01 · 开发：教师在线纠错

任务状态：已完成；已记录阶段 5；已处理阶段 5，其中保留问题继续 4。

已处理包含“保留问题继续”，不表示没有剩余问题；流程完成也不表示视觉质量通过。

阶段 1 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：环境与视角：学生先建立房间、窗和基础灯光。教师依据真实渲染指出顶部黑带、远墙占幅过小和前景地面偏大；学生只改相机。复核认为黑带消失、远墙比例改善，但左侧开口偏移和天花板辨识仍有问题。因此按额度带问题进入下一阶段。这些是教师过程判断，不是独立质量结论。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Room and viewpoint

目标：['room shell', 'window and door opening', 'camera', 'basic lighting']

完成范围：The room has its floor, walls, ceiling and beam, left glazed opening, approximate camera framing, and broad illumination.

教师纠错：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

Correct the viewpoint before advancing. Future furniture and decoration are outside this review.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
SceneActView camera and room-shell framing
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference fills the frame with the interior; the back-wall ceiling junction is near 28% image height and the visible back-floor junction near 73%.
实际渲染证据
A black strip appears above the shell. The back wall occupies only roughly 30–63% image height, making the room appear distant and the foreground floor disproportionately large.
建议修改
Use the single correction pass to bring the viewpoint forward into the shell and adjust framing so the back wall occupies more image height, the opening sits farther right, and the ceiling fills the upper edge.
需要保持
Preserve upright verticals, the left glazing, beige right wall, gray floor, and overhead beam.
修改后检查
Compare normalized back-wall corners and opening position against the reference; confirm no exterior black strip remains and the ceiling panel is visible below the beam.
不确定性
Camera position, aim, and room proportions jointly affect these landmarks; the reference does not determine unique dimensions or focal length. No extra view is requested.
记录状态
new
学生修改决定与建议落实情况（英文原文）

教师复核：保留未解决问题并继续

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The camera correction improves interior coverage and back-wall scale, but opening placement and ceiling-panel visibility remain unresolved. Advance with the issue recorded; the single correction pass is complete.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
SceneActView camera and room-shell framing
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference has a broad left foreground wall, glazing around 23–32% image width, and a visible ceiling panel below the beam.
实际渲染证据
The black upper strip is gone and the back-floor junction has moved from roughly 63% to 75% image height, improving back-wall scale. However, the glazing now reaches the left edge instead of moving right, the foreground wall is largely lost, and the ceiling panel is not distinguishable.
建议修改
Record the remaining framing mismatch and advance; no further correction is requested within this verification pass.
需要保持
Preserve the improved back-wall scale, removal of the black strip, upright verticals, room shell, and left glazing.
修改后检查
Carry forward the unresolved opening position and ceiling-panel visibility as framing dependencies for subsequent stages.
不确定性
Noise and bright ceiling values limit assessment of the panel. This view cannot uniquely determine camera parameters or room dimensions. No extra view is requested.
记录状态
partially_resolved
全部增量构建、错误和真实代码 diff

对象变化表只测量已记录的字段，不是完整的非目标保持证明。未覆盖的项目包括部分修改器参数、ColorRamp 内部值、UV、灯光尺寸及着色设置等；请同时检查真实补丁与渲染。失败 inventory 中的 patch_running 是未完成快照，实际执行状态以 result.json 和 build_process.json 为准。

+from sceneact import *
+
+scene = Scene('Office room')
+
+# The open front of the room faces the camera; the back wall is at positive Y.
+room = Asset('Room shell')
+room.include('floor', box((6.4, 8.2, 0.12), center=(0, 0.0, -0.06), color=(0.37, 0.39, 0.38)))
+room.include('back wall', box((6.4, 0.16, 3.35), center=(0, 4.0, 1.675), color=(0.86, 0.87, 0.85)))
+room.include('right wall', box((0.16, 8.0, 3.35), center=(3.2, 0, 1.675), color=(0.70, 0.61, 0.55)))
+# Split the left wall around the full-height glazed opening.
+room.include('left wall front', box((0.16, 3.4, 3.35), center=(-3.2, -2.3, 1.675), color=(0.86, 0.86, 0.84)))
+room.include('left wall rear', box((0.16, 1.25, 3.35), center=(-3.2, 3.375, 1.675), color=(0.86, 0.86, 0.84)))
+room.include('left opening header', box((0.16, 3.35, 0.42), center=(-3.2, 1.025, 3.14), color=(0.86, 0.86, 0.84)))
+room.include('ceiling', box((6.4, 8.0, 0.12), center=(0, 0, 3.41), color=(0.89, 0.89, 0.87)))
+room.include('ceiling beam', box((6.4, 0.46, 0.43), center=(0, -1.8, 3.135), color=(0.87, 0.87, 0.85)))
+room.include('back skirting', box((6.25, 0.06, 0.13), center=(0, 3.88, 0.065), color=(0.26, 0.27, 0.26)))
+room.include('left front skirting', box((0.06, 3.35, 0.13), center=(-3.08, -2.3, 0.065), color=(0.26, 0.27, 0.26)))
+room.include('left rear skirting', box((0.06, 1.18, 0.13), center=(-3.08, 3.38, 0.065), color=(0.26, 0.27, 0.26)))
+room.include('right skirting', box((0.06, 7.9, 0.13), center=(3.08, 0, 0.065), color=(0.26, 0.27, 0.26)))
+scene.include('room', room)
+
+opening = Asset('Left glazed opening')
+opening.include('glass', box((0.035, 3.25, 2.82), center=(-3.19, 1.025, 1.46), color=(0.77, 0.86, 0.87)))
+for y, name in [(-0.59, 'front jamb'), (2.64, 'rear jamb'), (0.98, 'center mullion')]:
+    opening.include(name, box((0.12, 0.075, 2.9), center=(-3.10, y, 1.48), color=(0.91, 0.92, 0.90)))
+opening.include('top frame', box((0.12, 3.34, 0.09), center=(-3.10, 1.025, 2.91), color=(0.91, 0.92, 0.90)))
+opening.include('bottom frame', box((0.12, 3.34, 0.09), center=(-3.10, 1.025, 0.055), color=(0.91, 0.92, 0.90)))
+opening.include('blind housing', box((0.20, 3.48, 0.16), center=(-3.05, 1.025, 3.02), color=(0.85, 0.84, 0.81)))
+scene.include('glazed opening', opening)
+
+# A flush ceiling light contributes a recognizable architectural feature.
+fixture = Asset('Ceiling light panel')
+fixture.include('rim', box((1.05, 0.80, 0.035), center=(-0.3, 2.05, 3.335), color=(0.82, 0.83, 0.81)))
+fixture.include('diffuser', box((0.95, 0.70, 0.018), center=(-0.3, 2.05, 3.31), color=(1.0, 1.0, 0.98)))
+scene.include('ceiling light', fixture)
+
+realize(scene)
+set_view((0, -7.8, 2.05), (0, 2.4, 1.55), lens=26, sensor=36)
+area_emitter((0, -2.0, 3.05), power=500, size=5, target=(0, 2, 0.8))
+area_emitter((-2.8, 1.0, 2.5), power=420, size=3.0, target=(0.5, 2.3, 1.0))
+area_emitter((0, 2.0, 3.22), power=200, size=1.8, target=(0, 2.0, 0))

阶段 2 · 已完成

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：主体办公家具：学生在同一场景加入桌子、两把访客椅、办公椅与右侧柜体。教师指出椅子下部软包离地太低，露出的金属支杆过短。学生局部调整软包底部和支杆；新渲染送回教师，教师认为支杆更清楚、椅背顶部及底座位置保持，允许继续。细小底座和地面接触仍受预览噪声影响。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Office furniture

目标：['desk', 'visitor chairs', 'office chair', 'right wall cabinetry']

完成范围：The main white desk, two green visitor chairs, rear office chair, and wood wall cabinetry are present in their reference positions.

教师纠错：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

All four furniture targets are present. One correction to visitor-chair pedestal proportions would improve this stage before advancing.

发现编号
S2-01
对象或关系
Both visitor chairs: upholstery-to-pedestal proportions
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference chairs have substantial exposed metal stems beneath their upholstered bodies, with clearly spread cross bases.
实际渲染证据
Both rendered chairs have upholstery extending close to the floor, leaving very short visible stems and compressed-looking bases.
建议修改
Use the single correction to raise the seat and lower upholstery edges and lengthen the exposed pedestal stems, keeping the chair-back tops near their current height.
需要保持
Keep two green chairs in front of the white desk, their rounded backs, and the rear office chair and right-wall cabinetry.
修改后检查
Confirm both chairs show a clear stem between upholstery and cross base, with bases resting on the floor and proportions closer to the reference.
不确定性
Render noise limits base detail. This is a visible proportion judgment, not an inference of physical dimensions; no extra view is requested.
记录状态
new
学生修改决定与建议落实情况（英文原文）
{
  "decision": "apply",
  "code": "import bpy\nfrom mathutils import Matrix\n\n# Shorten each upholstered back from below while retaining its top height.\nfor name in ('upholstered back', 'upholstered back.001'):\n    obj = bpy.data.objects.get(name)\n    if obj:\n        top = 1.38\n        obj.matrix_world = Matrix.Translation((0, 0, top)) @ Matrix.Diagonal((1, 1, 0.76, 1)) @ Matrix.Translation((0, 0, -top)) @ obj.matrix_world\n\n# Raise the seats and arms to expose more of each pedestal.\nfor name in ('seat cushion', 'arm left', 'arm right', 'seat cushion.001', 'arm left.001', 'arm right.001'):\n    obj = bpy.data.objects.get(name)\n    if obj:\n        obj.matrix_world = Matrix.Translation((0, 0, 0.20)) @ obj.matrix_world\n\n# Extend each stem upward from its existing foot; leave the cross bases on the floor.\nfor name in ('central stem', 'central stem.001'):\n    obj = bpy.data.objects.get(name)\n    if obj:\n        bottom = 0.12\n        obj.matrix_world = Matrix.Translation((0, 0, bottom)) @ Matrix.Diagonal((1, 1, 1.59, 1)) @ Matrix.Translation((0, 0, -bottom)) @ obj.matrix_world\n",

阶段 3 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：植物与设施：首建加入盆栽、暖气、顶灯和墙画。教师发现清单中存在的前景植物并未出现在画面内，后方植物也过于稀疏；学生移动前景植物并补充叶片轮廓。复核看到前景植物已进入左下角、暖气仍可辨认，但后方冠幅和密度仅部分接近原图，因此保留未解决问题。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Plants and fixtures

目标：['potted plants', 'radiator', 'ceiling light', 'wall art']

完成范围：Both plants, the rear radiator, ceiling light panel, and framed artwork establish the room’s secondary silhouettes.

教师纠错：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

Radiator, artwork, and ceiling panel are now visible. Prioritize one plant silhouette correction before advancing.

发现编号
stage3_plants
对象或关系
Foreground ficus (obj_00538) and rear ficus foliage (obj_00537)
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
A large leafy plant crops into the lower-left foreground; the rear plant has a dense, broad canopy beside the desk.
实际渲染证据
No foreground plant is visible. The rear plant reads as exposed, fanned stems with sparse tiny leaves.
建议修改
Use the single correction to bring the foreground plant into the lower-left crop and thicken both foliage silhouettes with broader, overlapping leaf clusters.
需要保持
Keep the rear pot beside the radiator, its separation from the desk, and the existing furniture and fixture placement.
修改后检查
Verify that both plants are visible, the foreground foliage frames the lower-left edge, and the rear canopy reads as leafy while leaving the radiator identifiable.
不确定性
The inventory includes a foreground plant, but this view cannot establish whether it is outside the frame or occluded. Render noise limits fine leaf assessment; no extra view is required.
记录状态
new
学生修改决定与建议落实情况（英文原文）
{
  "decision": "apply",
  "code": "import bpy\nimport math\nimport random\nfrom mathutils import Vector\n\n# Move the existing foreground plant into the camera's lower-left field of view.\nforeground = bpy.data.objects.get('Foreground ficus')\nif foreground:\n    foreground.location.x += 1.05\n    foreground.location.y += 0.15\n\nleaf_materials = [bpy.data.materials.get('Leaf green %d' % i) for i in range(4)]\n\ndef add_foliage(name, parent, center, radius, count, seed, length_range):\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    verts, faces, indices = [], [], []\n    cx, cy, cz = center\n    for i in range(count):\n        # An irregular oval crown with foliage distributed throughout its volume.\n        z = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)\n        theta = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)\n        radial = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - z*z)) * rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)\n        p = Vector((cx + radius[0]*radial*math.cos(theta),\n                    cy + radius[1]*radial*math.sin(theta),\n                    cz + radius[2]*z))\n        direction = Vector((rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.9)))\n        if direction.length < 0.01:\n            direction = Vector((1, 0, 0))\n        direction.normalize()\n        side = direction.cross(Vector((0, 0, 1)))\n        if side.length < 0.01:\n            side = direction.cross(Vector((0, 1, 0)))\n        side.normalize()\n        normal = direction.cross(side).normalized()\n        length = rng.uniform(*length_range)\n        width = length * rng.uniform(0.28, 0.42)\n        # Folded lance-shaped leaves remain individual editable polygons in one mesh.\n        points = [p - direction*length*0.50,\n                  p - direction*length*0.12 + side*width*0.50,\n                  p + direction*length*0.50,\n                  p - direction*length*0.12 - side*width*0.50,\n                  p + normal*length*0.06]\n        base = len(verts)\n        verts.extend([tuple(v) for v in points])\n        faces.extend([(base, base+1, base+4), (base+1, base+2, base+4),\n                      (base+2, base+3, base+4), (base+3, base, base+4)])\n        indices.extend([rng.randrange(4)] * 4)\n    mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name + ' mesh')\n    mesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)\n    mesh.update()\n    obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)\n    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.link(obj)\n    if parent:\n        obj.parent = parent\n    for mat in leaf_materials:\n        if mat:\n            mesh.materials.append(mat)\n    if len(mesh.materials):\n        for poly, idx in zip(mesh.polygons, indices):\n            poly.material_index = idx % len(mesh.materials)\n    return obj\n\nrear = bpy.data.objects.get('Rear ficus')\nadd_foliage('Rear ficus fuller canopy', rear, (-1.17, 3.47, 1.47), (0.55, 0.39, 0.64), 260, 431, (0.12, 0.23))\nadd_foliage('Foreground ficus fuller canopy', foreground, (-2.95, -2.15, 1.37), (0.78, 0.54, 0.63), 300, 732, (0.18, 0.33))",

  "actual_executed_code": "import bpy\nimport math\nimport random\nfrom mathutils import Vector\n\n# Move the existing foreground plant into the camera's lower-left field of view.\nforeground = bpy.data.objects.get('Foreground ficus')\nif foreground:\n    foreground.location.x += 1.05\n    foreground.location.y += 0.15\n\nleaf_materials = [bpy.data.materials.get('Leaf green %d' % i) for i in range(4)]\n\ndef add_foliage(name, parent, center, radius, count, seed, length_range):\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    verts, faces, indices = [], [], []\n    cx, cy, cz = center\n    for i in range(count):\n        # An irregular oval crown with foliage distributed throughout its volume.\n        z = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)\n        theta = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)\n        radial = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - z*z)) * rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)\n        p = Vector((cx + radius[0]*radial*math.cos(theta),\n                    cy + radius[1]*radial*math.sin(theta),\n                    cz + radius[2]*z))\n        direction = Vector((rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.9)))\n        if direction.length < 0.01:\n            direction = Vector((1, 0, 0))\n        direction.normalize()\n        side = direction.cross(Vector((0, 0, 1)))\n        if side.length < 0.01:\n            side = direction.cross(Vector((0, 1, 0)))\n        side.normalize()\n        normal = direction.cross(side).normalized()\n        length = rng.uniform(*length_range)\n        width = length * rng.uniform(0.28, 0.42)\n        # Folded lance-shaped leaves remain individual editable polygons in one mesh.\n        points = [p - direction*length*0.50,\n                  p - direction*length*0.12 + side*width*0.50,\n                  p + direction*length*0.50,\n                  p - direction*length*0.12 - side*width*0.50,\n                  p + normal*length*0.06]\n        base = len(verts)\n        verts.extend([tuple(v) for v in points])\n        faces.extend([(base, base+1, base+4), (base+1, base+2, base+4),\n                      (base+2, base+3, base+4), (base+3, base, base+4)])\n        indices.extend([rng.randrange(4)] * 4)\n    mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name + ' mesh')\n    mesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)\n    mesh.update()\n    obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)\n    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.link(obj)\n    if parent:\n        obj.parent = parent\n    for mat in leaf_materials:\n        if mat:\n            mesh.materials.append(mat)\n    if len(mesh.materials):\n        for poly, idx in zip(mesh.polygons, indices):\n            poly.material_index = idx % len(mesh.materials)\n    return obj\n\nrear = bpy.data.objects.get('Rear ficus')\nadd_foliage('Rear ficus fuller canopy', rear, (-1.17, 3.47, 1.47), (0.55, 0.39, 0.64), 260, 431, (0.12, 0.23))\nadd_foliage('Foreground ficus fuller canopy', foreground, (-2.95, -2.15, 1.37), (0.78, 0.54, 0.63), 300, 732, (0.18, 0.33))"

+def material(name, color, roughness=0.7):
+    m = bpy.data.materials.get(name) or bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    m.diffuse_color = (*color, 1)
+    m.use_nodes = True

+def group(name):
+    o = bpy.data.objects.new(name, None)
+    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.link(o)
+    return o
+
+def cube(name, loc, size, mat, parent):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=1, location=loc)
+    o = bpy.context.object
+    o.name = name
+    o.dimensions = size
+    bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(location=False, rotation=False, scale=True)
+    o.data.materials.append(mat)
+    o.parent = parent
+    return o
+
+def cylinder_between(name, a, b, radius, mat, parent, vertices=8):
+    a, b = Vector(a), Vector(b)
+    d = b-a
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(vertices=vertices, radius=radius, depth=d.length, location=(a+b)/2)
+    o = bpy.context.object
+    o.name = name
+    o.rotation_euler = d.to_track_quat('Z','Y').to_euler()
+    o.data.materials.append(mat)
+    o.parent = parent
+    return o
+
+def pot(name, x, y, scale, parent):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cone_add(vertices=24, radius1=.19*scale, radius2=.25*scale, depth=.46*scale, location=(x,y,.23*scale))
+    o=bpy.context.object
+    o.name=name+' tapered pot'
+    o.data.materials.append(potmat)
+    o.parent=parent
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(vertices=24, radius=.26*scale, depth=.055*scale, location=(x,y,.45*scale))
+    o=bpy.context.object
+    o.name=name+' pot rim'
+    o.data.materials.append(potmat)
+    o.parent=parent
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(vertices=24, radius=.225*scale, depth=.01*scale, location=(x,y,.482*scale))
+    o=bpy.context.object
+    o.name=name+' soil'
+    o.data.materials.append(soil)
+    o.parent=parent
+
+def plant(name, x, y, scale, height, spread, branches):
+    root=group(name)
+    pot(name,x,y,scale,root)
+    base=Vector((x,y,.46*scale))
+    verts=[]
+    faces=[]
+    indices=[]
+    for j in range(branches):
+        angle=2*math.pi*j/branches + random.uniform(-.3,.3)
+        h=height*random.uniform(.66,1.0)
+        radial=spread*random.uniform(.42,.93)
+        tip=Vector((x+math.cos(angle)*radial,y+math.sin(angle)*radial,h))
+        fork=base.lerp(tip,.49)+Vector((random.uniform(-.05,.05),random.uniform(-.05,.05),0))
+        cylinder_between(name+' trunk %02d'%j,base,fork,.025*scale,bark,root)
+        cylinder_between(name+' branch %02d'%j,fork,tip,.011*scale,bark,root)
+        for k in range(12):
+            t=random.uniform(.35,1.02)
+            stem=fork.lerp(tip,t)
+            ang=angle+random.uniform(-1.35,1.35)
+            length=random.uniform(.12,.23)*scale
+            out=Vector((math.cos(ang),math.sin(ang),random.uniform(-.2,.55))).normalized()
+            end=stem+out*length
+            side=Vector((-out.y,out.x,random.uniform(-.15,.15))).normalized()*length*.28
+            mid=stem.lerp(end,.52)+Vector((0,0,.025*scale))
+            n=len(verts)
+            verts.extend([stem,mid+side,end,mid-side,mid+Vector((0,0,.02*scale))])
+            faces.extend([(n,n+1,n+4),(n+1,n+2,n+4),(n+2,n+3,n+4),(n+3,n,n+4)])
+            indices.extend([random.randrange(4)]*4)
+    mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name+' leaf mesh')
+    mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
+    mesh.materials.clear()
+    for m in greens: mesh.materials.append(m)
+    mesh.update()
+    obj=bpy.data.objects.new(name+' individual leaves',mesh)
+    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.link(obj)
+    obj.parent=root
+    for poly, idx in zip(mesh.polygons,indices): poly.material_index=idx
+
+potmat=material('Plant pots - charcoal',(.025,.035,.033),.38)
+soil=material('Dark potting soil',(.075,.055,.035))
+bark=material('Plant woody stems',(.19,.12,.065))
+greens=[material('Leaf green %d'%i,c) for i,c in enumerate([(.035,.18,.025),(.055,.28,.035),(.10,.34,.045),(.025,.13,.018)])]

+
+frame=material('Artwork dark wood frame',(.18,.085,.045))

+def add_foliage(name, parent, center, radius, count, seed, length_range):
+    rng = random.Random(seed)
+    verts, faces, indices = [], [], []
+    cx, cy, cz = center
+    for i in range(count):
+        # An irregular oval crown with foliage distributed throughout its volume.

+        direction.normalize()

+            side = direction.cross(Vector((0, 1, 0)))
+        side.normalize()
+        normal = direction.cross(side).normalized()
+        length = rng.uniform(*length_range)
+        width = length * rng.uniform(0.28, 0.42)
+        # Folded lance-shaped leaves remain individual editable polygons in one mesh.

+                  p - direction*length*0.12 + side*width*0.50,

+                  p + normal*length*0.06]
+        base = len(verts)
+        verts.extend([tuple(v) for v in points])
+        faces.extend([(base, base+1, base+4), (base+1, base+2, base+4),
+                      (base+2, base+3, base+4), (base+3, base, base+4)])
+        indices.extend([rng.randrange(4)] * 4)
+    mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name + ' mesh')
+    mesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)
+    mesh.update()
+    obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)
+    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.link(obj)
+    if parent:
+        obj.parent = parent
+    for mat in leaf_materials:
+        if mat:
+            mesh.materials.append(mat)
+    if len(mesh.materials):
+        for poly, idx in zip(mesh.polygons, indices):
+            poly.material_index = idx % len(mesh.materials)
+    return obj

阶段 4 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：桌面细节：首份补丁因为显示器底座的圆角半径超过厚度限制而执行失败，上一成功场景保留。学生用一次技术修复改正参数并真实渲染后，教师才开始视觉反馈：显示器与浅色背景混在一起。学生另用一次视觉纠错调整背板对比和朝向；复核认为面板更容易辨识，但支架与桌面的接触仍看不清，带问题继续。技术修复没有被计作视觉纠错。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Desktop details

目标：['computer', 'desk accessories', 'small decor', 'architectural trim']

完成范围：The monitor and desktop accessories are in place, along with visible window hardware, baseboards, and other small architectural details.

教师纠错：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The desktop additions are only faintly readable. Prioritize the monitor silhouette in the available correction.

发现编号
stage4_monitor
对象或关系
computer (obj_00626), especially monitor back and stand
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference has a clearly outlined gray monitor above the desk, right of the office chair, with an angled back and visible support.
实际渲染证据
In attachment 2, no clear monitor silhouette separates from the pale area right of the office chair; the newly visible pink flowers are easier to identify than the computer.
建议修改
Use the single correction to make the monitor readable: adjust its back material contrast and orientation so the gray panel and thin edge separate clearly from the wall, with its stand seated on the desktop.
需要保持
Preserve the desk, seating, accessory arrangement, architectural trim, and camera.
修改后检查
Confirm that the monitor reads clearly above the desk and right of the office chair in the same view.
不确定性
Noise and washed-out tones obscure the monitor; this view cannot distinguish material washout from occlusion reliably. No extra view was observed.

new

  "code": "import bpy\nfrom math import radians\nfrom mathutils import Matrix, Vector\n\n# Give the existing monitor housing its own contrasting finish.\nhousing = bpy.data.objects.get('silver monitor back')\nbezel = bpy.data.objects.get('monitor lower bezel')\nmat = bpy.data.materials.get('Monitor back - contrasting silver')\nif mat is None:\n    mat = bpy.data.materials.new('Monitor back - contrasting silver')\n    mat.diffuse_color = (0.31, 0.32, 0.33, 1.0)\n    mat.use_nodes = True\n    shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')\n    if shader:\n        shader.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (0.31, 0.32, 0.33, 1.0)\n        shader.inputs['Metallic'].default_value = 0.25\n        shader.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.48\n\ndef descendants(root):\n    for child in root.children:\n        yield child\n        yield from descendants(child)\n\nif housing:\n    for obj in descendants(housing):\n        if obj.type == 'MESH':\n            obj.data.materials.clear()\n            obj.data.materials.append(mat)\n\n# Turn and gently tilt the panel as a unit, keeping its support on the desk.\npivot = Vector((1.48, 2.22, 1.06))\ntransform = (Matrix.Translation(pivot) @ Matrix.Rotation(radians(-8), 4, 'Z') @ Matrix.Rotation(radians(-7), 4, 'X') @ Matrix.Translation(-pivot))\nfor panel in (housing, bezel):\n    if panel:\n        panel.matrix_world = transform @ panel.matrix_world\n",

The single correction makes the monitor identifiable. Advance with its support still difficult to verify.

发现编号
stage4_monitor
对象或关系
computer (obj_00626), especially monitor back and stand
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference monitor has a distinct gray back, angled outline, and visible support right of the office chair.
实际渲染证据
Attachment 2 now shows a gray monitor panel clearly separating from the wall, unlike attachment 3. Its lower edge and support still blend into the pale desktop.
建议修改
No further patch requested; carry the remaining support-readability issue forward.
需要保持
Preserve the improved panel contrast, desk, seating, accessories, trim, and camera.
修改后检查
Panel visibility is improved; support contact with the desktop remains unverified.
不确定性
Render noise and pale tones limit assessment of the stand and thin edges. No extra view was observed or requested.
记录状态
partially_resolved

One correction pass is useful: prioritize framing and tonal separation, then verify the actual resulting render.

development_observation

teacher_observation

The reference has a broad left foreground wall, larger furniture in the lower right, and a ceiling light near the upper fifth.
实际渲染证据
The left wall is largely absent, the furniture appears smaller and farther away, and the ceiling light sits near the upper third. A dark strip remains across the top.
建议修改
Use the single correction pass to adjust camera framing against these visible landmarks and ensure ceiling coverage across the top edge.
需要保持
Keep both visitor chairs before the desk, the monitor on its right, and the rear plant beside the radiator.
修改后检查
Compare wall width, chair size, ceiling-light position, and top-edge coverage in the resulting render.
不确定性
This view does not determine a unique focal length or room dimensions. The dark strip's cause is uncertain; the reported ceiling extension has no clear visible effect.

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The single correction visibly improved chair colors and changed framing, but composition and render clarity remain unresolved. Advance with these issues recorded.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
SceneActView; room and furniture framing
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference shows a broad left foreground wall, furniture filling the lower right, and a modest foreground plant.
实际渲染证据
Furniture is larger and farther right than in attachment 3, but the broad left wall remains absent. The foreground plant now dominates the lower left and obscures the radiator; the right chair is clipped. The dark top strip persists.
建议修改
Retain as an unresolved framing limitation; no further patch in this verification.
需要保持
Keep the desk behind both visitor chairs and the monitor to the office chair's right.
修改后检查
Verification confirms changed framing, but wall width, plant coverage, chair cropping, and ceiling coverage still miss the reference.
不确定性
The dark strip's cause remains uncertain. A single view does not establish unique camera parameters or room dimensions. No extra view requested.
记录状态
partially_resolved
发现编号
F2
对象或关系
Global lighting; floor, right wall, and upholstery appearance
问题来源
development_observation
规则来源
teacher_observation
参考图证据
The reference separates the grey floor, white desk, taupe wall, and darker olive right chair with soft directional shading.
实际渲染证据
Attachment 2 now shows a distinctly darker olive right chair and warmer right wall. The floor remains pale and heavy noise still obscures textures and subtle shadows.
建议修改
Accept the visible tonal improvement and carry the remaining appearance limitation forward without another patch.
需要保持
Preserve the chair color distinction, warm right wall, and bright white desk.
修改后检查
Verification supports improved color separation, but does not confirm clean surface detail or reference-level floor contrast.
不确定性
Render noise prevents reliable assessment of oak grain, fabric texture, and contact shadows.
记录状态
partially_resolved

B．教学经验怎样进入学生

下列文件只在实际产生后展示。通用策略来自开发轨迹；测试中发现的问题必须另有测试原图与当前渲染证据。这是检查策略提炼，不是模型权重训练、教师自进化或已证实的能力提升。

已知执行边界：冻结规则 R6 要求保留未解决问题，但运行器只在同一阶段的复核中传入 previous_findings；后续阶段首次自查收到的该字段为空。实际图像仍随场景保留，但没有结构化的跨阶段未解决问题记忆。因此不能把规则文本中的要求写成已经实现的跨阶段问题追踪能力。

中文展示摘要：共冻结6条规则：R1限定本阶段检查范围（既有通用规则）；R2比较构图地标并复查视角变化的影响；R3区分对象清单中的存在与画面中的可辨认性；R4检查部件比例和支撑并保留锚点；R5比较相对明暗、明确噪声下无法判断的细节；R6依据真实修改后结果更新问题状态、保留部分解决及退化。R2–R6来源于这次开发轨迹。第一次提炼中的技术参数规则引用了并不存在的教师反馈编号，经过唯一一次来源修正后被撤下，原记录保留。办公室的具体物体、坐标、颜色和完整构造方案没有传入测试。该Python入口只组织学生看图检查提示，不自主测量几何，不训练权重，也不是教师自进化。

实际共享自检查规则（英文原文／实际记录）打开原始文件
Apply these questions using only the current supplied evidence. Record uncertainty instead of guessing.

1. **Scope and authority — preexisting_general_rule:** What is completed at this stage, what operation is permitted, and is visual correction available? Review that scope, preserve unrelated work, and respect the execution contract.
2. **Framing — development_derived:** How do normalized landmark positions, projected sizes, margins, and edge coverage compare with the reference? After a framing change, recheck cropping and occlusion across the completed scope.
3. **Presence and visibility — development_derived:** Which required elements exist in the inventory, and which are actually recognizable in the render? Compare silhouette coverage and density; distinguish absence from uncertain visibility.
4. **Part relationships — development_derived:** Do visible part proportions, gaps, overlaps, and support contacts match the reference? Identify anchors to preserve during a local correction and mark obscured contacts unverified.
5. **Tonal readability — development_derived:** Do important adjacent surfaces and silhouettes remain distinguishable? Compare relative brightness and shading before judging fine detail, and state what noise or washout prevents assessing.
6. **Verification and carryover — development_derived:** What actually changed, what does the resulting render confirm, and what regressed? Revisit prior findings, label each resolved, partially resolved, unresolved, or unverified, and carry remaining issues forward when correction is unavailable.

实际执行的学生检查入口（英文原文／实际记录）打开原始文件
import json


def build_review_prompt(context):
    return (
        "Review the current supplied evidence in English. Apply the frozen questions below; do not infer answers from the questions.\n\n"
        "1. Scope and authority [preexisting_general_rule]: What is completed at this stage, what operation is permitted, and is visual correction available? Review that scope, preserve unrelated work, and respect the execution contract.\n"
        "2. Framing [development_derived]: How do normalized landmark positions, projected sizes, margins, and edge coverage compare with the reference? After a framing change, recheck cropping and occlusion across the completed scope.\n"
        "3. Presence and visibility [development_derived]: Which required elements exist in the inventory, and which are actually recognizable in the render? Compare silhouette coverage and density; distinguish absence from uncertain visibility.\n"
        "4. Part relationships [development_derived]: Do visible part proportions, gaps, overlaps, and support contacts match the reference? Identify anchors to preserve during a local correction and mark obscured contacts unverified.\n"
        "5. Tonal readability [development_derived]: Do important adjacent surfaces and silhouettes remain distinguishable? Compare relative brightness and shading before judging fine detail, and state what noise or washout prevents assessing.\n"
        "6. Verification and carryover [development_derived]: What actually changed, what does the resulting render confirm, and what regressed? Revisit prior findings, label each resolved, partially resolved, unresolved, or unverified, and carry remaining issues forward when correction is unavailable.\n\n"

阶段 1 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：环境与视角：学生用冻结规则R2、R5检查当前厨房原图和实际渲染，发现左上角大块黑色背景、木地板颜色过浅。它修改房间覆盖范围及地板外观；复核认为墙体顶部和木板质感有所变化，但左上黑区及色调差异仍在，带问题进入下一阶段。全程由Terra完成，无教师看图。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Room and viewpoint

目标：['floor', 'walls', 'camera', 'basic lighting']

完成范围：A simple room shell, wood-toned floor, camera, and lighting establish the reference viewpoint and composition.

学生实际自查：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The stage-one shell and lighting are present, but the room does not cover the frame and the floor reads substantially paler than the reference. One visual correction is available.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
Room shell and camera framing
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The room fills the reference frame to every edge; no exterior background is visible.
实际渲染证据
Black background occupies the upper-left region, extending about 31% across the top and down to about 59% of image height at the left edge. The visible wall also ends at about 10% of image height.
建议修改
Extend the room shell beyond the camera view, or adjust the camera and wall coverage together, so the rendered room fills the frame.
需要保持
Keep the continuous floor, visible wall-floor contact, and broadly downward-looking perspective.
修改后检查
Confirm that no black exterior remains at any edge and recheck floor coverage and wall-floor contact.
不确定性
The reference furnishings obscure much of the wall and floor boundary, so the exact shell dimensions and camera position cannot be determined.
记录状态
new
发现编号
F2
对象或关系
Floor appearance
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R5
参考图证据
The exposed floor is a medium warm brown with visible variation and wood grain.
实际渲染证据
The floor is much paler and more uniform, with long uninterrupted parallel seams; individual wood grain and staggered board ends are not readily visible.
建议修改
Adjust the floor material and lighting balance toward a warmer, deeper wood tone with restrained variation and visible board character.
需要保持
Preserve the floor coverage and readable plank direction.
修改后检查
Check that the floor remains distinct from the pale wall and that its warmth and variation are visible without obscuring seams.
不确定性
Lighting and material both affect the rendered color; the image alone does not isolate their contributions. Furnishings obscure part of the reference floor.
记录状态
new
学生修改决定与建议落实情况（英文原文）
{
  "decision": "apply",
  "code": "import bpy\nimport random\n\n# Extend the existing back wall beyond the camera's field of view.\nwall = bpy.data.objects.get('Box.042')\nif wall:\n    wall.dimensions.x = 30.0\n    wall.dimensions.z = 12.0\n    wall.location.z = 6.0\n\n# Give the existing plank materials a warm, subtly varied wood appearance.\nfor i in range(5):\n    mat = bpy.data.materials.get('SceneActSurface_' + str(i))\n    if not mat:\n        continue\n    mat.use_nodes = True\n    nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes\n    links = mat.node_tree.links\n    bsdf = nodes.get('Principled BSDF')\n    if not bsdf:\n        continue\n    for link in list(bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].links):\n        links.remove(link)\n    geometry = nodes.new('ShaderNodeNewGeometry')\n    geometry.label = 'World position for wood grain'\n    stretch = nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')\n    stretch.operation = 'MULTIPLY'\n    stretch.inputs[1].default_value = (10.0, 0.65, 3.0)\n    noise = nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')\n    noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value = 3.0\n    noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3.0\n    noise.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.68\n    ramp = nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')\n    shade = (i - 2) * 0.012\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = 0.22\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (0.19 + shade, 0.075 + shade * 0.5, 0.027 + shade * 0.2, 1)\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = 0.78\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (0.48 + shade, 0.255 + shade, 0.105 + shade * 0.5, 1)\n    links.new(geometry.outputs['Position'], stretch.inputs[0])\n    links.new(stretch.outputs['Vector'], noise.inputs['Vector'])\n    links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs['Fac'])\n    links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'], bsdf.inputs['Base Color'])\n    bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.62\n\n# Short cross-grain joints mark staggered board ends without changing coverage.\nseam_mat = bpy.data.materials.get('Floor board end joints')\nif seam_mat is None:\n    seam_mat = bpy.data.materials.new('Floor board end joints')\n    seam_mat.diffuse_color = (0.16, 0.085, 0.043, 1.0)\n    seam_mat.use_nodes = True\n    seam_bsdf = seam_mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')\n    seam_bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (0.16, 0.085, 0.043, 1.0)\n    seam_bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.8\nrandom.seed(19)\nverts = []\nfaces = []\nfor i in range(42):\n    x = -5.04 + i * 0.24\n    offset = random.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)\n    for j in range(-3, 4):\n        y = j * 1.65 + offset\n        if not -4.98 < y < 4.98:\n            continue\n        n = len(verts)\n        verts.extend([(x-0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y+0.003,0.001), (x-0.116,y+0.003,0.001)])\n        faces.append((n,n+1,n+2,n+3))\nmesh = bpy.data.meshes.new('Staggered floor joint mesh')\nmesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)\nmesh.update()\njoints = bpy.data.objects.new('Staggered floor board joints', mesh)\nbpy.context.collection.objects.link(joints)\njoints.data.materials.append(seam_mat)\njoints.parent = bpy.data.objects.get('Room.001')\n",

  "actual_executed_code": "import bpy\nimport random\n\n# Extend the existing back wall beyond the camera's field of view.\nwall = bpy.data.objects.get('Box.042')\nif wall:\n    wall.dimensions.x = 30.0\n    wall.dimensions.z = 12.0\n    wall.location.z = 6.0\n\n# Give the existing plank materials a warm, subtly varied wood appearance.\nfor i in range(5):\n    mat = bpy.data.materials.get('SceneActSurface_' + str(i))\n    if not mat:\n        continue\n    mat.use_nodes = True\n    nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes\n    links = mat.node_tree.links\n    bsdf = nodes.get('Principled BSDF')\n    if not bsdf:\n        continue\n    for link in list(bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].links):\n        links.remove(link)\n    geometry = nodes.new('ShaderNodeNewGeometry')\n    geometry.label = 'World position for wood grain'\n    stretch = nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')\n    stretch.operation = 'MULTIPLY'\n    stretch.inputs[1].default_value = (10.0, 0.65, 3.0)\n    noise = nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')\n    noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value = 3.0\n    noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3.0\n    noise.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.68\n    ramp = nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')\n    shade = (i - 2) * 0.012\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = 0.22\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (0.19 + shade, 0.075 + shade * 0.5, 0.027 + shade * 0.2, 1)\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = 0.78\n    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (0.48 + shade, 0.255 + shade, 0.105 + shade * 0.5, 1)\n    links.new(geometry.outputs['Position'], stretch.inputs[0])\n    links.new(stretch.outputs['Vector'], noise.inputs['Vector'])\n    links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs['Fac'])\n    links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'], bsdf.inputs['Base Color'])\n    bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.62\n\n# Short cross-grain joints mark staggered board ends without changing coverage.\nseam_mat = bpy.data.materials.get('Floor board end joints')\nif seam_mat is None:\n    seam_mat = bpy.data.materials.new('Floor board end joints')\n    seam_mat.diffuse_color = (0.16, 0.085, 0.043, 1.0)\n    seam_mat.use_nodes = True\n    seam_bsdf = seam_mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')\n    seam_bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (0.16, 0.085, 0.043, 1.0)\n    seam_bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.8\nrandom.seed(19)\nverts = []\nfaces = []\nfor i in range(42):\n    x = -5.04 + i * 0.24\n    offset = random.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)\n    for j in range(-3, 4):\n        y = j * 1.65 + offset\n        if not -4.98 < y < 4.98:\n            continue\n        n = len(verts)\n        verts.extend([(x-0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y+0.003,0.001), (x-0.116,y+0.003,0.001)])\n        faces.append((n,n+1,n+2,n+3))\nmesh = bpy.data.meshes.new('Staggered floor joint mesh')\nmesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)\nmesh.update()\njoints = bpy.data.objects.new('Staggered floor board joints', mesh)\nbpy.context.collection.objects.link(joints)\njoints.data.materials.append(seam_mat)\njoints.parent = bpy.data.objects.get('Room.001')\n"

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The executed revision improved wall coverage and floor character, but the upper-left black area and a floor appearance difference remain. Visual correction is unavailable, so advance with these issues carried forward.

F1
对象或关系
Room shell and camera framing
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The room fills the reference frame without a black exterior area.
实际渲染证据
The wall now reaches the top edge, but black remains over the upper-left region, about 29% of the frame width at the top and 59% of its height at the left edge. The floor and wall-floor contact remain visible.
建议修改
Carry forward the need for room coverage across the upper-left area; no further visual correction is available in this verification.
需要保持
Preserve the continuous floor, wall-floor contact, and downward-looking viewpoint.
修改后检查
No further patch cycle is permitted at this stage.
不确定性
Furnishings obscure much of the reference wall-floor boundary, so exact shell dimensions and camera position remain uncertain.
记录状态
partially_resolved
发现编号
F2
对象或关系
Floor appearance
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R5
参考图证据
The exposed reference floor is warm medium brown with wood variation and visible board joints.
实际渲染证据
Compared with the previous checkpoint, the current floor is warmer and more textured, and staggered end joints are visible. It remains lighter and more uniform than much of the reference floor.
建议修改
Carry forward the remaining floor tone and variation difference; no further visual correction is available in this verification.
需要保持
Preserve floor coverage, readable plank direction, and visible staggered joints.
修改后检查
No further patch cycle is permitted at this stage.
不确定性
Reference furnishings and shadows obscure or darken parts of the floor, and the image alone does not separate material from lighting effects.
记录状态
partially_resolved

+from sceneact import *
+
+scene = Scene('Room')
+room = Asset('Room shell')
+
+# Warm, narrow floorboards establish the direction and color of the room.
+floor_colors = [(0.48, 0.32, 0.19), (0.53, 0.36, 0.22), (0.57, 0.39, 0.24), (0.50, 0.33, 0.20), (0.55, 0.37, 0.23)]
+board_width = 0.24
+for i in range(42):
+    x = -5.04 + i * board_width
+    room.include('Floorboard_%02d' % i, box(size=(board_width - 0.004, 10.0, 0.09), center=(x, 0, -0.045), color=floor_colors[i % len(floor_colors)]))
+
+wall_color = (0.79, 0.83, 0.79)
+trim_color = (0.25, 0.17, 0.11)
+room.include('Back wall', box(size=(10.2, 0.12, 3.8), center=(0, 3.7, 1.9), color=wall_color))
+room.include('Right wall', box(size=(0.12, 10.0, 3.8), center=(5.05, -1.3, 1.9), color=wall_color))
+room.include('Back baseboard', box(size=(10.0, 0.08, 0.22), center=(0, 3.60, 0.11), color=trim_color))
+room.include('Right baseboard', box(size=(0.08, 7.2, 0.22), center=(4.95, 0.05, 0.11), color=trim_color))
+scene.include('Room', room)
+realize(scene)
+
+set_view((4.5, -7.5, 3.65), (0.0, 0.45, 1.05), lens=36)
+area_emitter((0.0, -2.8, 5.5), power=1000, size=5.0, target=(0, 0.7, 0.4))
+area_emitter((-3.5, 0.0, 3.8), power=500, size=4.0, target=(0, 1.5, 0.8))

+        if not -4.98 < y < 4.98:
+            continue
+        n = len(verts)
+        verts.extend([(x-0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y-0.003,0.001), (x+0.116,y+0.003,0.001), (x-0.116,y+0.003,0.001)])
+        faces.append((n,n+1,n+2,n+3))
+mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new('Staggered floor joint mesh')
+mesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)
+mesh.update()
+joints = bpy.data.objects.new('Staggered floor board joints', mesh)
+bpy.context.collection.objects.link(joints)
+joints.data.materials.append(seam_mat)
+joints.parent = bpy.data.objects.get('Room.001')

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：工作岛：学生用R2指出工作岛在画面中偏小、短端面过窄，决定仅调整相机并保留岛体几何。补丁执行成功，却使画面几乎全黑、工作岛不可辨认。学生实际复看后没有声称修好，而是用R6新增“可见性退化”问题，指出清单中岛体仍存在。按单次视觉纠错额度带问题继续，未由外层工程师回滚或补一份更好结果。这是必须保留的负面案例。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Central work island

目标：['island countertop', 'island cabinet body', 'island supports']

完成范围：The large rectangular wood countertop and its darker supporting cabinet establish the central silhouette, overhang, and floor contact.

学生实际自查：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The countertop, cabinet body, and support posts are present in the inventory and recognizable in the render. One visual correction is available; the largest observed mismatch is the island's small projected size and different viewing angle.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
Island framing and viewing angle
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The island spans approximately 70% of the image width and 55% of its height. Its countertop has substantial visible depth, and the broad end panel is prominent in the foreground.
实际渲染证据
The island spans approximately 49% of the image width and 30% of its height. The long cabinet face dominates, while the short end is a narrow sliver; much more empty floor and wall surrounds it.
建议修改
Adjust the camera position, aim, and lens to bring the island closer in frame and make its short end more visible, while retaining the complete countertop and floor contacts.
需要保持
Preserve the island geometry, its countertop overhang, cabinet-to-top relationship, floor contacts, and existing room geometry.
修改后检查
Compare the island's projected width, height, tabletop depth, and visible end face with the reference; check that the countertop and supports remain uncropped.
不确定性
The reference and render may differ in both camera angle and island proportions, so the exact camera adjustment cannot be inferred from the images alone. Some support contacts are obscured.
记录状态
new

+def block(name, location, size, material, bevel=0.0):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=1, location=location)
+    obj = bpy.context.object
+    obj.name = name
+    obj.dimensions = size
+    bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(location=False, rotation=False, scale=True)
+    obj.parent = root
+    obj.data.materials.append(material)
+    if bevel:
+        mod = obj.modifiers.new('Soft worn edges', 'BEVEL')
+        mod.width = bevel
+        mod.segments = 2
+        obj.modifiers.new('Weighted normals', 'WEIGHTED_NORMAL')
+    return obj
+
+def bar(name, a, b, radius, material):
+    midpoint = (Vector(a) + Vector(b)) / 2
+    delta = Vector(b) - Vector(a)
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(vertices=12, radius=radius, depth=delta.length, location=midpoint)
+    obj = bpy.context.object
+    obj.name = name
+    obj.rotation_euler = delta.to_track_quat('Z', 'Y').to_euler()
+    obj.parent = root
+    obj.data.materials.append(material)
+    return obj
+

阶段 3 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：沿墙柜体：学生先加入左侧展示柜和右侧上下柜，并因相机位于右墙外而关闭该墙的渲染可见性，让场景重新出现在画面中。自查随后发现柜体压在画面右侧、下柜被工作岛遮挡、木色过浅；本阶段视觉纠错只改相机。复核认为柜体覆盖和右侧裁切改善，但左侧黑边、遮挡与色调仍有差异。此阶段自查两次声称原图和渲染宽高比不同；实际尺寸核对表明原图1280×960、预览768×576，均为4:3，因此该诊断说法错误；原文仍保留。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Perimeter cabinetry

目标：['left display cabinet', 'right wall cabinet', 'right base cabinet']

完成范围：The tall glazed cabinet on the left and the upper and lower cabinetry along the right wall establish the surrounding furniture arrangement.

学生实际自查：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The cabinetry is present in the inventory and recognizable in the render, but its framing and visibility differ substantially from the reference. One visual correction is available; no prior findings were supplied.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
Framing and visibility of perimeter cabinetry
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The left display cabinet spans roughly x=0.04–0.46 of the reference, while the right cabinetry begins around x=0.50; substantial portions of both are visible behind the island.
实际渲染证据
The left cabinet is compressed into roughly x=0.49–0.76, its lower section is mostly hidden by the island, and the right cabinetry starts around x=0.72 and is heavily cropped at the right edge. A black region occupies much of the upper left.
建议修改
Use the available visual correction to adjust the camera framing and viewpoint toward the reference, bringing more of both perimeter cabinet groups into view and reducing the exposed black region.
需要保持
Keep the existing cabinet assemblies, their parts, and the island intact; preserve the visible distinction between upper and lower right cabinets.
修改后检查
Compare cabinet extents, island overlap, right-edge cropping, and room coverage in the resulting render across the completed scope.
不确定性
The reference and render have different aspect ratios, and some lower cabinet contacts are obscured by the island. The exact camera adjustment cannot be determined from the images alone.
记录状态
new
发现编号
F2
对象或关系
Perimeter cabinet tonal appearance
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R5
参考图证据
The cabinet wood and right countertop read as dark brown against the pale wall and lighter island top; glazing shows varied reflections.
实际渲染证据
The visible perimeter cabinet frames and fronts appear pale tan, and the glazing reads as almost uniform pale panels. The right countertop is also much lighter than the reference.
建议修改
Carry the tonal mismatch forward for assessment after the framing correction; no separate change is proposed in this correction.
需要保持
Keep cabinet frames, door divisions, and hardware recognizable.
修改后检查
Reassess wood, countertop, and glazing contrast in the corrected view.
不确定性
Lighting, exposure, and the limited visible area may contribute; the render does not establish a unique material cause.

+    mat = bpy.data.materials.get(name) or bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    mat.diffuse_color = (*color, 1.0)
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
+    bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (*color, 1.0)
+    bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = roughness
+    bsdf.inputs['Metallic'].default_value = metallic
+    return mat

阶段 4 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：柜体细部：学生加入门框、抽屉、五金和右侧凹位部件，自查仍认为工作岛占幅偏小、左下柜被遮住。一次相机纠错后，学生估计岛台从约49%画宽扩大到59%，但左边黑区和遮挡未消失，右侧柜体又被裁切。因此只记为部分解决；这些占比是学生视觉估计，不是独立像素测量。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Cabinet joinery

目标：['doors and drawers', 'frames and panels', 'glass', 'hardware', 'trim']

完成范围：Visible cabinet divisions, recessed panels, glazed door frames, handles, and base trim make the furniture groups recognizable while preserving their established proportions.

学生实际自查：请求局部修订

展开发现、证据和决定（英文原文）

The completed joinery is recognizable, but the current framing makes it much smaller and obscures more of the left lower cabinet than the reference. One visual correction is available.

发现编号
F1
对象或关系
Framing and visibility of cabinet joinery
问题来源
test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The island countertop spans approximately 70% of the image width, and the furniture fills most of the frame. Its near end and the left cabinet's lower doors are visible.
实际渲染证据
The island countertop spans approximately 49% of the image width, with substantial empty floor and a black wedge along the left edge. The island also obscures nearly all of the left cabinet's lower doors, although the inventory contains their panels and frames.
建议修改
Adjust the camera framing and viewpoint to bring the furniture closer in frame and improve visibility of the cabinet fronts.
需要保持
Preserve the established furniture proportions, cabinet divisions, glazing, hardware, and the right recess uprights.
修改后检查
Compare the new render's island size and cabinet visibility with the reference; check that the furniture remains recognizably framed and that the black edge is eliminated.
不确定性
The reference and render use different viewing angles, so projected widths alone do not establish a geometry mismatch. The left lower doors exist in the inventory but their visible details cannot be assessed through the island.
记录状态
new

F1
对象或关系
Framing and visibility of cabinet joinery

test_observation
规则来源
R2
参考图证据
The island countertop spans about 70% of the image width; the left cabinet's lower door and the island's near end are visible.
实际渲染证据
The current countertop spans about 59% of the image width, up from approximately 49% in the previous checkpoint. The black wedge remains at the left edge, the island still hides nearly all of the left lower doors, and the right cabinetry is now cropped at the image edge.
建议修改
Carry the remaining framing and visibility issue forward; no further visual correction is available in this review.
需要保持
Preserve the established furniture proportions, cabinet divisions, glazing, hardware, and recess uprights.
修改后检查
No additional patch or render is permitted in this verification cycle.
不确定性
The different viewing angles affect projected dimensions. The inventory contains the left lower door panels and frames, but their visible details and obscured contacts cannot be verified in this view.

+# Butt the horizontal door rails into the stiles. The existing overlapping
+# faces occupy the same plane and produce dark artifacts at their corners.
+for obj in bpy.data.objects:
+    if obj.type != 'MESH':
+        continue
+    n = obj.name
+    if n.startswith('Door horizontal frame'):
+        obj.dimensions.x = 0.83
+    elif n.startswith('Left glazed door crossbar'):
+        obj.dimensions.x = 0.48
+    elif n.startswith('Left glazed door') and ' rail' in n:
+        obj.dimensions.x = 0.48
+    elif n.startswith('Left lower door') and ' rail' in n:
+        obj.dimensions.x = 1.08
+    elif n.startswith('Right base door') and ' rail' in n:
+        obj.dimensions.x = 0.84
+    elif n.startswith('Right glazed door') and ' rail' in n:
+        obj.dimensions.x = 0.86
+
+# Give the existing glazing a restrained translucent, reflective finish.
+glass = bpy.data.materials.get('Display cabinet muted glazing')
+if glass:
+    glass.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = glass.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
+    if bsdf:
+        bsdf.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (0.48, 0.66, 0.64, 1.0)
+        bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.22
+        if 'Transmission Weight' in bsdf.inputs:
+            bsdf.inputs['Transmission Weight'].default_value = 0.58
+        elif 'Transmission' in bsdf.inputs:
+            bsdf.inputs['Transmission'].default_value = 0.58
+        bsdf.inputs['IOR'].default_value = 1.45
+
+wood = bpy.data.materials.get('Perimeter cabinet stained oak')
+inset = bpy.data.materials.get('Perimeter cabinet inset oak') or wood
+
+def add_box(name, center, size, material, parent):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=1, location=center)
+    obj = bpy.context.object
+    obj.name = name
+    obj.dimensions = size
+    if material:
+        obj.data.materials.append(material)
+    if parent:
+        obj.parent = parent
+        obj.matrix_parent_inverse = parent.matrix_world.inverted()
+    return obj

+left = bpy.data.objects.get('Left tall display cabinet')
+right = bpy.data.objects.get('Right wall and base cabinets')
+# Shelves sit behind the glass and supply the horizontal depth visible in the reference.

阶段 5 · 保留未解决问题并继续

本阶段来源：阶段原始 JSON · 步骤与失败 CSV

中文展示摘要：外观和整体回看：学生在首次结果中指出左侧黑色楔形、岛台视角及占幅不符、台面偏浅和玻璃偏青。本阶段选择只扩展背景墙，保留其他问题。第一次视觉补丁调用因工作区路由失败未返回程序；用户确认连接恢复后，原请求唯一重试成功。新渲染复核认为左上黑楔形消失，但左侧下方仍有黑色区域，构图和外观问题未解决，按额度结束。随后只提高到冻结的最终分辨率和采样渲染，没有额外教师或质量评审。测试共22次CLI（21完成、1路由失败），10次构建成功、11帧渲染，教师/Astra调用为0。

阶段计划：目标与已完成范围（英文原文）

Appearance and global check

目标：['wood materials', 'glass appearance', 'lighting balance', 'scene-wide verification']

完成范围：Wood tones and surface variation distinguish the countertop, cabinetry, and floor; a final render checks framing, visibility, contacts, and tonal readability across the completed scene, with obscured details recorded as uncertain.

+# Procedural finishes remain editable and do not depend on image files.
+def wood_material(name, dark, light, mapping_scale, noise_scale=4.0, roughness=0.4):
+    mat = bpy.data.materials.get(name) or bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
+    nodes.clear()
+    links = mat.node_tree.links
+    out = nodes.new('ShaderNodeOutputMaterial')
+    bsdf = nodes.new('ShaderNodeBsdfPrincipled')
+    bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = roughness
+    tex = nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
+    mapping = nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
+    mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
+    mapping.inputs[1].default_value = mapping_scale
+    noise = nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
+    noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value = noise_scale
+    noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3.0
+    noise.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.7
+    ramp = nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = 0.24
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*dark, 1.0)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = 0.76
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*light, 1.0)
+    bump = nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
+    bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value = 0.12
+    bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value = 0.025
+    links.new(tex.outputs['Generated'], mapping.inputs[0])
+    links.new(mapping.outputs['Vector'], noise.inputs['Vector'])
+    links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs['Fac'])
+    links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'], bsdf.inputs['Base Color'])
+    links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'], bump.inputs['Height'])
+    links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'], bsdf.inputs['Normal'])
+    links.new(bsdf.outputs['BSDF'], out.inputs['Surface'])
+    return mat
+
+counter = wood_material('Finish - honey oak island top', (0.30, 0.145, 0.055), (0.58, 0.36, 0.16), (1.2, 22.0, 3.0), 3.0, 0.34)
+cabinet = wood_material('Finish - dark stained cabinet oak', (0.075, 0.036, 0.018), (0.24, 0.13, 0.065), (9.0, 4.0, 1.2), 3.2, 0.4)
+inset = wood_material('Finish - recessed oak panels', (0.065, 0.030, 0.015), (0.19, 0.095, 0.045), (9.0, 4.0, 1.2), 3.2, 0.44)
+backtop = wood_material('Finish - dark perimeter counter', (0.065, 0.038, 0.025), (0.18, 0.105, 0.065), (1.2, 18.0, 3.0), 3.0, 0.36)
+floors = []
+for i, factor in enumerate((0.91, 0.97, 1.02, 1.06, 0.95)):
+    floors.append(wood_material('Finish - floor oak %02d' % i, tuple(v * factor for v in (0.25, 0.13, 0.065)), tuple(v * factor for v in (0.48, 0.29, 0.145)), (5.0, 0.65, 4.0), 3.0, 0.48))
+
+glass = bpy.data.materials.get('Finish - reflective muted glass') or bpy.data.materials.new('Finish - reflective muted glass')
+glass.use_nodes = True
+p = glass.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
+if p:
+    p.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (0.36, 0.49, 0.47, 1.0)
+    p.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = 0.22
+    p.inputs['Metallic'].default_value = 0.16
+    if 'Coat Weight' in p.inputs:
+        p.inputs['Coat Weight'].default_value = 0.35
+
+def assign(obj, material):
+    if obj.type == 'MESH':
+        if obj.data.materials:
+            obj.data.materials[0] = material
+        else:
+            obj.data.materials.append(material)
+
+for obj in bpy.data.objects:
+    if obj.type != 'MESH':
+        continue
+    parent_name = obj.parent.name if obj.parent else ''
+    if parent_name.startswith('Floorboard_'):
+        try:
+            assign(obj, floors[int(parent_name.split('_')[-1]) % len(floors)])
+        except ValueError:
+            pass
+    elif obj.name == 'Solid oak countertop':
+        assign(obj, counter)
+    elif obj.name == 'Right dark countertop':
+        assign(obj, backtop)
+    elif 'glazed door' in obj.name.lower() and 'inset' in obj.name.lower():
+        assign(obj, glass)
+    elif parent_name in {'Central work island', 'Left tall display cabinet', 'Right wall and base cabinets'}:
+        original = obj.data.materials[0].name if obj.data.materials else ''
+        if original in {'Island dark stained oak', 'Perimeter cabinet stained oak'}:
+            assign(obj, cabinet)
+        elif original in {'Island recessed panels', 'Perimeter cabinet inset oak'}:
+            assign(obj, inset)
+
+# Extend the existing wall beyond the camera view to remove the black void at the left edge.
+wall = bpy.data.objects.get('Box.042')
+if wall:
+    wall.scale.x *= 1.5

服务中断与一次恢复控制

以下恢复记录仅授权已保存路由故障的唯一剩余重试，不重抽成功回复、不改变图片、模型、effort、冻结策略或预算。原失败仍计入调用。连接恢复确认不等于已验证服务健康；是否成功以对应真实 session.json 为准。

保留的原始服务暂停证据 · 原失败调用 · 唯一恢复尝试 · 恢复控制工程修订及适用范围

继续未完成步骤使用 python launch.py resume，默认保留成功缓存。只有新的、尚有一次机会的路由故障经用户确认恢复后才使用 resume --service-recovered；本轮已有的恢复确认不能重复增加机会。完整说明见 README。

steps.csv 同时列出构建与 CLI 行；实际图片附件归属相应 CLI 行，build_context_images 单独保留构建元数据的上下文图片，不把 Blender 执行说成模型看图。真实先后以 events.jsonl 为准。
